(4) B4: M50_PT_Exp4 at f = 0.040625 over 21.39 s under Stage 11's kinetic draws of rank 5, 50, 151 and 196 (Stage 3's draws 188, 83, 40 and 184, D111 (1)) and with the aged SEI (the SEI's initial amount 0, D66); then, at the posterior mean, the rate laws evaluated at T + s (D176 (1)): s = 5, 10, 15 K and on until a run reaches C8 (at most 60 K), then s 2.5 K below the first that does. The least shift is the smaller of the two that reaches C8, and its C8 time is recorded.
(5) V4: B1's M50_PT_Exp4 runs at the two ends of its bracket, each made again with the mesh refined by 1.5 and with every step limit halved (D176 (3)).
(6) The list before any record holds the first run of every chain: B1's eight, B2's two, B3's first, B4's five and its first shift (5 K), seventeen runs; with the rules' parameters (the failure rule of D176 (4) among them) it is hashed (`stage15_partb.list_hash`): SHA-256 99cbbf7a58c1bfaec6b69cbd633a5ec3c074d8e812ea124be31b5b119333ce11.

D176. Tests, checks and the runs (brief, checks).
(1) The three-dimensional loop (`src/stage15_model.py`). `run_resolved15` is `stage9_model.run_resolved` (the compiled kernel) operation for operation, with four additions, each off by default: the stop at C8 (the first step at which the mean reaches T_L, the crossing taken linear in the mean within the step); the shift of the rate laws (B4: the kinetics' start-of-step rates and sub-steps are given T + s and their result shifted back, so that each node's reaction heat is the rate law's at T + s and the temperature it heats is the node's own; with s = 0 the kinetics are called as they are); the accounts every 0.5 s (both neighbours' terms, doubled to the whole cell as the records' are); and the mean at every step beside the probes. `run_lumped15` is `stage9_model.run_lumped` with the same shift and accounts. The hold (B3) is Stage 9's constant power over a span reaching past the run's end, from t0 = 21.39 s, so what it delivers is P (t - t0).
(2) Tests (`src/test_stage15.py`, run before the predictions; all six pass): `run_resolved15` with its additions off equals `stage9_model.run_resolved` bitwise (3 s of a made-up trigger ramp to 600 C, the central contact, a 2,000 J triangular source, and the aged SEI with no source); with the stop at the mean it ends at the first step whose mean reaches the level and repeats the unstopped run up to it; its accounts' last row is the record's total; the shift evaluates the rate laws at T + s and returns the node's own temperature, and a positive shift speeds the lumped reaction; `run_lumped15` equals `stage9_model.run_lumped` bitwise; the hold delivers P (t - t0); V3; and Part B's chains run to their ends with a made-up model in place of the runs (B1's four brackets, B3's match, B4's least shift, V4's four runs, and a chain stopped by a run that failed twice; no run made). Before the predictions the three-dimensional model ran only the five repeats of D172 (archived configurations) and two runs of the driver of 2 s and 0.5 s of model time, outside every registered configuration, to test the pulse and hold's two sources and the refined mesh's probes (they found the hold's span set by the resolved run's end rather than the lumped twin's 900 s, corrected before any registered run; no registered quantity).
(3) Checks. V1 and V3 are made (D170 (2), D173 (3)). V2: every Stage 15 record's energy accounts close to under 1e-9 of the heat taken in (resolved and lumped), and every delivery equals its pulse's integral to the run's end, the stop included (to 1e-9 relative). V4: B1's M50_PT_Exp4 runs at the two ends of its bracket made again with the mesh refined by 1.5 (82,309 nodes) and with every step limit halved (`dt_scale` 0.5: the base step, the target heating and the acceptance of a reaction sub-step); acceptance: the outcome (C8 or none) does not change, and where there is C8 its time moves by less than 5 %. V5: after the scoring and before the note's Stage 15 lines are final, an independent read by a separate agent that has not seen the work being produced: every number of the note's Stage 15 lines, the table and the sentences recomputed from the records.
(4) The runs: on this machine (two cores, PARDISO), by two workers of `stage15_run.py work`, each next run read from the records by the registered rules (D175); a run that fails is made again at once, and a run that fails at both attempts is recorded as failed and stops its chain, which is reported (the brief's rule that two failed attempts at a step stop it; written into the driver on 18 November, before the predictions). Records go to `results/stage15_runs/` and series to `results/stage15_series/` (the 0.1 s histories, the mean and the five probes at every step, the lumped twin).

## Stage 15: the runs, the results and the texts, after the registration

D177. Part B's runs (brief; `src/stage15_run.py work`, `src/stage15_partb.py`). The 27 runs were made on this machine (two cores, PARDISO) by two workers from 05:13:22 to 07:25:54 UTC on 18 November, after the registration's push at 05:12:45 UTC (3f5e3b6; GitHub's record of the branch's pushes, refreshed with the last commit, is in `provenance/github_activity_stage15.json`); none failed. They are B1's nine, B2's two, B3 (b)'s two, B4's ten and V4's four. Each next run was read from the records by the registered rules (D175), so only the first seventeen were named before the runs: B1 needed one further run (M50_SP_Exp3 at 1.15 times the cross-section's value), B3 (b) one (10 W), B4's shift four beyond its registered first at 5 K (10, 15, 20 and 17.5 K), and V4 its four once B1's M50_PT_Exp4 bracket was found. On the base mesh a run took 240 to 512 s; V4's two on the refined mesh took 2,762 and 1,916 s, and its two with the step limits halved 676 and 472 s; 3.85 hours of the workers' time in all. The workers took V4's runs first once they were due (an order, not a result, `stage15_partb.pending`). Each worker stopped taking new runs after 50 minutes and ended when its run in hand did, the background job's limit being two hours, so that no run was cut short; the first was started again at once (its next run began at 06:06:50 UTC) and the second six minutes after its last run ended (its next run began at 06:15:00 UTC). Records are in `results/stage15_runs/`, series in `results/stage15_series/`, and the workers' logs in `results/stage15_logs/partb_w1.log` and `partb_w2.log`.

D178. Part B's results (`results/stage15_scorecard.json`, `results/stage15_paper_numbers.json`).
(1) B1, the three-dimensional E*(C8) with the 21.39 s pulse. M50_PT_Exp4: no C8 at 6,709 J (the mean peaks at 215.3 C at 223.0 s), C8 at 181.9 s at 7,416 J; the bracket (6,709, 7,416] and its midpoint 7,062 J, the cross-section's value, 1.49 times the clock's E*(C2) of 4,754 J. M50_PP_Exp2: no C8 at 6,511 J (215.8 C), C8 at 188.1 s at 7,197 J; 6,854 J, the cross-section's value, 1.72 times E*(C2). M50_SP_Exp3: no C8 at 3,351 and 3,704 J (the mean peaks at 215.9 and 234.1 C), C8 at 270.2 s at 4,057 J; 3,881 J, 1.10 times the cross-section's 3,528 J, where the lumped neighbour crosses with no delivery (E*(C2) = 0). M50_ST_Exp3: no C8 at 9,767 J (the mean peaks at 211.2 C at 21.4 s, the pulse's end, and falls), C8 at 61.5 s at 10,795 J; 10,281 J, the cross-section's value, 0.85 times E*(C2) of 12,030.5 J. In M50_ST_Exp3 the lumped twin crosses in neither run (it releases 351 and 583 J of reaction heat), the one test of the four in which the resolved cell runs away where the lumped one does not. With the three strong triggers the lumped twin crosses in every run, at 106.1 to 127.5 s, and releases 22.4 to 22.6 kJ, where the resolved cell has released 4,796, 4,703 and 4,075 J by its C8 (6,307 J in M50_ST_Exp3). The resolved cell's reaction heat passes A0.2's mark of 11.3 kJ in no Stage 15 run; the most is 6,410 J, by 900 s in B4's run with the rate laws shifted by 17.5 K (D178 (4)), and the most by a C8 is that 6,307 J.
(2) The far face (A0.2, D171 (2)) in the crossing runs at the brackets' upper ends: M50_PT_Exp4 (7,416 J), its rise slowing below 0.5 K/s and its jump beginning at 173.0 s at 234.1 C, 36.8 K above 197.3 C and 47.6 K above the 186.5 C at which the same rule finds the measured record's own jump; M50_PP_Exp2 (7,197 J), at 179.0 s at 234.1 C; M50_SP_Exp3 (4,057 J), no jump before C8, and 230.0 C twenty seconds before it; M50_ST_Exp3 (10,795 J), 261.3 C twenty seconds before C8 at 61.5 s. B3 (a), Stage 10's rule against M50_PT_Exp4's measured history over 0 to 181.9 s (the window ends at C8): the root mean square of the difference 51.4 K, the model's far face 41.6 K above the record on average and 77.2 K above it at the window's end (244.6 against 167.4 C), its rise over the last 60 s 34.8 K against the record's 10.6 K; it passes 150 C at 24.2 s, the record at 84.0 s. The far face is below the record until 17.5 s and above it from then on.
(3) B3 (b), the pulse and the hold. With 5,045 J over 21.39 s and then 12.5 W, C8 comes at 255.9 s, 15.6 s before 271.5 s; with 10 W, at 369.3 s. The search so ended in two runs with the neighbouring powers 10 and 12.5 W bracketing 271.5 s ("bracketed"). By C8 the hold had added 2,931 J at 12.5 W (7,976 J in all) and 3,479 J at 10 W. With 12.5 W the far face's jump begins at 247.5 s at 233.6 C (with 10 W the rule finds none, and the far face reads 241.3 C twenty seconds before C8). Stage 10's rule over 0 to 255.9 s for the 12.5 W run: root mean square 33.1 K, the model 20.4 K above the record on average and 61.7 K above it at 255.9 s (244.4 against 182.7 C); it is below the record until 52.5 s (135.7 against 141.0 C at 45 s) and above it from then on, and it passes 150 C at 62.1 s against 84.0 s. The delivery's shape is a choice: the hold brings the cell's own runaway near the measured time, and the far face then rises faster and higher than the record.
(4) B4, the kinetics, at M50_PT_Exp4's 5,045 J over 21.39 s: no C8 under the draws of rank 5, 50, 151 and 196 (the mean peaks at 191.9, 191.4, 192.4 and 191.2 C) nor with the aged SEI (188.3 C), against 190.6 C at the posterior mean (D170 (3) 1). The lumped twin runs away under the draws of rank 5 and 50 (at 191.0 and 196.5 s) and not under the other two or with the aged SEI. With the rate laws evaluated at T + s: no C8 at s = 5, 10, 15 and 17.5 K (the mean peaks at 191.9, 194.2, 200.6 and 221.1 C), and C8 at 270.3 s at 20 K, the least shift on the registered steps (the shift at which the cell begins to run away lies between 17.5 and 20 K), where the lumped twin crosses at 122.5 s. The posterior's ignition temperatures span 6.14 K over 95 % of the 200 draws (215.26 to 221.39 C, D140 (1)).
(5) B2, M50_PT_Exp4 with 240 s of constant power. At 6,209 J (f = 0.05) no C8: the mean peaks at 241.4 C at 339.8 s, 0.2 K above the archived run's last reading (241.2 C at 332 s), and falls to 116.6 C by 900 s; the hot spot peaks at 253.5 C and never reaches the level; the cell releases 5,885 J; the lumped twin crosses at 177.4 s. At 5,821 J no C8 (216.4 C), the lumped twin crossing at 184.1 s.

D179. The scorecard (`results/stage15_scorecard.json`; each verdict by the rule registered in `predictions_stage15.md`, computed by `stage15_score.scorecard`, unchanged since the registration). P1 held: the cross-section's E*(C8) lies within 15 % of the three-dimensional midpoint in all four of B1's tests (0.0 % in M50_PT_Exp4, PP_Exp2 and ST_Exp3, by construction: their first two runs, 5 % either side of the cross-section's value, bracket the threshold, and the bracket's midpoint is that value; 9.1 % below it in M50_SP_Exp3, whose threshold lies 5 to 15 % above the cross-section's value). P2 held: 7,062 J for M50_PT_Exp4. P3 held: three of the four exceed E*(C2) by 25 % or more (M50_PT_Exp4 1.49 times, PP_Exp2 1.72 times, SP_Exp3 3,881 J against 0), and M50_ST_Exp3 does not (0.85 times), as its expectation allowed. P4 held: C8 at 181.9 s in M50_PT_Exp4's run at the bracket's upper end. P5 held: the far face's jump begins at 234.1 C, 36.8 K above 197.3 C. P6 held: the search ended bracketed by 10 and 12.5 W, both between 5 and 25 W. P7 held: none of B4's five runs reaches C8, and the least shift is 20 K. P8 failed: the 240 s run at 6,209 J, carried to 900 s, does not run away (D178 (5)); the cross-section had stated no C8 (its mean peaking at 220.5 C, D174 (6)), and the expectation, likely, rested on the archived run's rise at its end. Seven held and one failed. Through Stage 15, with Stage 14's six on its own branch, the study has committed 98 predictions, of which 56 held and 42 failed (39 through Stage 13, two of Stage 14's and one of Stage 15's).

D180. Part C (brief; `src/stage15_score.py`).
(1) Part C1 (`results/stage15_table.csv`): for the nine propagated tests and each of the four deliveries, the delivered energy each criterion needs to give the measured time t_m, the time it gives, and the energy delivered by then (a constant power over 240 or 360 s has not been fully delivered when its crossing comes first). The whole-cell clock's (C2) is Stage 12's reading with the 21.39 s pulse (D123 (3)) and Stage 9's with the long deliveries (D91); the hot spot's (C1) is Stage 9's; their crossing times are read from the archived run records at those energies, except the clock's seven matched times with the pulse, which are Stage 12's as its paper numbers give them (`results/stage12_paper_numbers.json`, to 0.1 s; within 0.05 s of the records). The cell's own runaway (C8) is the cross-section's (D174 (5)), with the three-dimensional E*(C8) beside it where Part B made it. Each energy is given as a share of 76.74 kJ (Stage 12's fixed ejected energy, the Databank's median, D122) and with the paths of Stage 14 whose corrected upper bound within the delivery's duration reaches it (branch stage14 at 026b9c0, `results/stage15_stage14_bounds.csv`). With the 21.39 s pulse the cell's own runaway at t_m needs 4,582 to 7,649 J in the five matched tests, 1.28 to 2.19 times the clock's energy, and 17.4 times in M50_SP_Exp3 (4,582 against 264 J); over 120 s, 4,064 to 7,743 J, 1.62 to 2.73 times (18.6 in M50_SP_Exp3). Over 360 s C8 comes before the delivery ends in all eight matched cases, and over 240 s in six of the eight (M50_PT_Exp4 and PP_Exp4 run away after it, at 271.4 and 272.8 s); the cell has had 4,179 to 8,116 J by its C8 (13,844 J in M50_ST_Exp3 over 240 s). Across the four deliveries the energy delivered by C8 at t_m is 4,064 to 8,116 J in the 25 matched cases outside M50_ST_Exp3, where the clock's is 219 to 5,962 J by its time in 27. The energies the cell's own runaway needs are 5.3 to 25.4 % of 76.74 kJ, and each is at most 0.37 of the least of Stage 14's corrected bounds within its delivery's duration (Stage 14's path A1, radiation from the jet or flame of a cap-end vent, 27.9 kJ within 21.39 s): every determinable path's bound reaches every criterion's energy, so the bounds do not choose between the criteria.
(2) Part C2 (`results/stage15_nodelivery.csv`): the sixteen tests with no delivery at the low, central and high contacts, C1 and C2 from Stage 7's runs, and C8 from the archive's continued runs where they exist (11 at the central contact, 3 at the high), from Stage 7's runs where the hot spot never reaches the level within 900 s (the mean cannot pass the hottest node; 16, 5 and 3), and from the cross-section otherwise (10 at the high contact, D174 (6)). No test's cell runs away by C8 at any contact, where the hot spot reaches the level in 0, 8 and 13 tests and the lumped neighbour in 0, 2 and 9 at the low, central and high contacts; nine tests propagated in the rig.
(3) Part C3 (`results/stage15_sentences.md`): two sentences for the manuscript's revision and one each for Professors Deng, Sapsis and Ghoniem, as proposals for the main chat, each with its numbers.

D181. Checks (brief, checks; `results/stage15_checks.json`). V1 and V3 were made before the predictions (D170 (2), D173 (3)). V2: in all 27 records the energy accounts close to within 1.6e-12 (resolved) and 2.0e-12 (lumped) of the heat taken in, and every delivery equals its pulse's integral to the run's end, the stop at C8 included, exactly. The check's code was corrected before it was first run on all the records: the records' sources do not carry their shape, and its first form took every source as triangular; it now builds each source from the record's own deliveries, as the run did. V4: M50_PT_Exp4's runs at the two ends of B1's bracket keep their outcomes on the refined mesh (82,309 nodes) and with every step limit halved; at the upper end C8 moves from 181.9 s to 184.6 s (1.5 %) and to 181.9 s (0.01 %), and at the lower end the mean's peak moves from 215.3 C to 214.7 and 215.3 C. V5: an independent read by a separate agent that had not seen the work being produced, made at 9f69150 (its report is `results/stage15_independent_read.md`). It reproduced the registration (both files pushed at 05:12:45 UTC, before the first run at 05:13:22 UTC, and unchanged since; the list's and the file's SHA-256), every Part B chain and its 27 runs with none missing or extra, C8 from each record's stored mean at every step (to 5.7e-14 s, at the code's level, the archive's T_L of 528.831575 K, which the texts round to 255.68 C; at 528.83 K exactly every C8 comes 0.0007 to 0.0043 s earlier and no outcome changes), every verdict, V2, V4, the far face's jump starts, the history metrics, all 36 rows of Part C1's table and all 48 of Part C2's, the archive's counts and spot checks of the five readings and A0.4's accounts. It reported 31 discrepancies, none moving a verdict, and each is corrected here or in the texts. (1) The history score's window ended at the end of the step in which C8 falls (182.0 s) instead of at C8 (181.9 s) as registered: corrected in `stage15_score.history_score`, B3 (a)'s root mean square is 51.4 K, not 51.5 K (D178 (2)), and B3 (b)'s values at its window's end 244.4 against 182.7 C (D178 (3)). (2) to (5) D178 and D180: the most reaction heat of any run, 6,410 J; the 10 W hold's energy by C8, 3,479 J; M50_ST_Exp3's E*(C2), 12,030.5 J; and over 240 s two of the eight matched cases run away after the delivery's end. (6) to (19) Statements of the README section, the note's lines and the sentences that the records did not support, or overstated: with no delivery the lumped twin does run away in two tests; the far face is below the record in the first 17.5 s (52.5 s with the hold), and it is the coolest of the five mid-length probes at its jump's start but not throughout, so the texts no longer call the difference a lower bound; the holds of 10 and 12.5 W bracket 271.5 s rather than reach it; four kinetic draws, not every draw; 151 central runs of the 199 continued; M50_SP_Exp3's threshold lies 5 to 15 % above the cross-section's value, its bracket's midpoint 10 %; the three-dimensional runs searched three of the fourteen tests with strong triggers; the clock's 0.26 to 6 kJ is the pulse's range; ten of the high contact's sixteen no-delivery outcomes rest on the cross-section alone; a bracket 10 % wide does not carry the precision first quoted for its midpoint; the least shift lies between 17.5 and 20 K; and two of the sentences' sources were not named. (20) to (26) D177's count of B4's runs beyond the first seventeen, the second worker's restart and the provenance file's reach; this entry's own V5 line; D180's source of the clock's matched times with the pulse; the labels of Part C, now written Part C1, C2 and C3 where they could be read as the criteria, Stage 14's path A1 named where it is used, and Part B's labels defined in the README; and the figure's caption. (27) to (30) Registered entries are not edited, so the read's corrections to them stand here: in D172 (2) the lumped twin lost 541 J more by convection and radiation in M50_PP_Exp3 (not 540 J), stored 28.2 K more (not 28.1 K), and in M50_ST_Exp2 the resolved mean is 58.7 C at 29 s, reaching 60 C at 29.8 s; in D172 (3) the repeats were 2.3 to 3.7 times faster, and the 107 runs without C8 include four of the cloud machine's (the laptop's 103 took a median of 646 s); in D174 (1) the 58 runs crossing within 30 s had 15.4 to 134.8 kJ (M50_PT_Exp2's 240 and 360 s runs at fractions up to 1.0 among them), not 15 to 118 kJ; and in D174 (5) M50_SP_Exp3's matched energy is 17.3 times the clock's 265.6 J (17.2 was from the rounded 266 J). (31) V3's figure, 7.4e-13 K, had no stored record: `src/test_stage15.py` prints it, and its output, run again after the read, is kept in `results/stage15_logs/test_stage15.log`.

D182. The texts and the files. `predictions_stage15.md`; `results/stage15_archive_table.csv`, `stage15_readings.json`, `stage15_v1.json`, `stage15_accounts.json`, `stage15_sizing.json`, `stage15_repeats/`, `stage15_cross_section.json` and `stage15_xs/`, `stage15_runs/` and `stage15_series/`, `stage15_scorecard.json`, `stage15_table.csv`, `stage15_nodelivery.csv`, `stage15_checks.json`, `stage15_paper_numbers.json`, `stage15_sentences.md`, `stage15_stage14_bounds.csv` and `stage15_logs/`; the figure `results/stage15_figure.pdf` (and `.png`): M50_PT_Exp4's resolved neighbour under the short delivery (5,045 J over 21.39 s, the repeat of D172 to 332 s), the two ends of B1's bracket, the pulse and the 12.5 W hold, and the 240 s delivery of 6,209 J, (a) its mean and (b) its far face with the measured history. A README section and a note section of six numbered lines (`note/neighbour_onset_note.md`, before "What it means for the thesis"; the earlier text unchanged), each written as "branch stage15, not released". Changed after the registration, none touching a run or a verdict's rule: Part C's table code (`stage15_score.c1_table`), rewritten to read the clock's and the hot spot's crossing times from the archived run records at their energies and to give the energy delivered by each time; V2's code (D181); the paper numbers (`stage15_score.paper_numbers`); the figure's labels; and the history score's window, which now ends at C8 as registered (D181). The disclosures: the hold's span corrected before any registered run (D176 (2)); the cross-section's base step switching at 30 s, not Stage 13's 60 s (D173 (3)); the jump rule's form, centred 2 s differences on a 0.5 s grid, chosen because it reproduces reading 5 (D171 (2)); the rule's own reading of the measured record, 186.5 C, against the brief's reference of 197.3 C (D171 (2)); the stand-in twenty seconds before C8 extended to the two cases the brief does not name, fixed before any run (D171 (2)); the five repeats of archived runs made before the predictions (D172 (1)); the cross-section's lean, late and cool in the parallel tests, by the heat their tabs carry (D174 (1)); Stage 9's rule at the measured times ending at the threshold in nine searches, eight of them within the rule's resolution (D174 (5)); and the background job's time limit, which stopped Part A once and the workers twice, each resuming from the records (D174, D177). `manuscript/data/predictions_by_stage.csv` lies under `manuscript/`, which this stage does not open: its Stage 15 row (eight committed, seven held, one failed) is left to the main chat. Nothing is merged, tagged or released; the expected version is 1.11.0, at the author's word from the main chat.

D183. The rebuilt manuscript (Brief 29, 20 November 2026; branch `manuscript-rebuild`). The manuscript of 4 November, submitted on 17 November with a shorter title and a rewritten supplement, is rebuilt in `manuscript/rebuild/` around Stages 13 to 15, on one question: how much heat must reach a neighbouring cell for it to run away, and can models built from published inputs say? No run is made and no results file is changed; `numbers_check.py` reproduced the earlier manuscript's numbers before any text was written (1,066 found and none missing; 1,267 with the supplement). (1) The order of the results, each from the stage that holds it: conduction and radiation from the trigger's body (Stage 12's capped runs and Stage 5's 5,248 runs); what each of the three readings needs with the 21.39 s pulse (the hottest point and the lumped neighbour from Stage 14's needs table, the resolved neighbour as a whole from Stage 15's B1 and the reactive cross-section); why they differ (Stage 13's skin and cross-section conduction models, the ignition temperature and its posterior span, Stage 15's B4 shifts); what the sixteen tests say (Stages 10 and 12, Stage 15's table at the measured times, the one published history with B3); what could deliver the heat (Stage 14's corrected bounds); and the reactive cross-section as a reduced model (Stage 15's Part A). (2) The labels: no stage number, decision number, file name or criterion label (C1, C2, C8, f*, E*) appears in the main text; the readings are named in words (the resolved neighbour's hottest point, the lumped neighbour, the resolved neighbour as a whole), the stages are the study's "steps", listed with their push dates in the supplement's Table S1, and the 98 registered predictions (56 held, 42 failed, 24 of the failures foreseen) are its Table S2, written by `src/make_rebuild_registry.py` from the predictions files and the scorecards. (3) Left out: Stages 1 to 4 (an earlier geometry, with a gap between the cells), the fractions of the ejected energy of Stages 8 to 11 (the new text gives energies), Stage 6's 150 C criterion and the exploratory runs. (4) The paper does not say which neighbour model is right: it gives what each needs, why, by how much they differ, where the measured record lies against each, and the measurement that would decide (the heat flux on the facing side, with the thermocouple's angle).

D184. The numbers (Brief 29, rule 4). `python3 manuscript/numbers_check.py --rebuild` reads the rebuilt manuscript's title, abstract, body with its captions, conclusions, appendices and declarations, its three tables, the highlights, the keywords, the five titles, the graphical abstract's labels (`ga_text.txt`, written by `src/make_rebuild_ga.py` as it draws them) and the letters (`letters/*.txt`), and matches every number to an entry of `manuscript/rebuild/number_sources.py`: a context, the number as written, a selector that names the file and the value it is read from, and a unit change (J to kJ, a fraction to per cent, or the magnitude where the text states the sign in words). The value rounded to the written precision must equal the number; a number with no entry fails, even where some value in the results happens to equal it. Selectors only pick a value, or the smallest or largest over named rows; none computes a quantity. Settings of the model are read from the code: the wedge sector's 45 degrees (`src/v12_mesh.py`), the reaction step limit of 1 K (`src/reactive.py`), the 3 % first step of Stage 13's searches and the 2 kJ shift of P10 (`src/stage13_inputs.py`), the aged SEI's zero (`src/stage15_inputs.py`), and the can's mass and specific heat (`manuscript/data/parameters.csv`, whose row gives the line of the two-cell study's code it is read from). Table 1 is checked cell by cell against the column each cell is made from. With `--supplement` the supplement's text and its three generated tables are checked too, by a match among the results files where no entry applies. The logs are `manuscript/rebuild/numbers_check.log` and `numbers_check_supplement.log`; `numbers_trace.json` holds every traced number with its sentence and source, and `numbered_sentences.md` (by `src/make_rebuild_sentences.py`) lists every numbered sentence with its sources, marking the numbers the submitted manuscript did not state. Changed while tracing, so that the text states no number that no file holds: the through-heated threshold's "10.5 % below the conduction model" (a ratio of two file values) became "falls below"; SP_Exp3's threshold "5 to 15 % above" the cross-section's value (the run design read as ratios) became "above both"; a fraction with no source "taken as 1" became "the whole is assumed"; the pair 4.66 and 5.04 kJ, called "the lumped neighbour's bracket" though Stage 12 narrowed that bracket to 4.66 to 4.85 kJ, became "either side of the lumped neighbour's threshold"; and, in the drafting before the check existed, the ratios of 1.08 to 6.16, 1.28 to 2.19 and 17.4, "90 s before", "above it from 17.5 s on", "6.1 to 12.7 %", "0.37 of the least bound", "69 to 81 %", "8 mm", "0.01 s" and the cross-section's agreement with A1 and A2 to 0.05 % and 0.73 were taken out or replaced by values a file holds (for SP_Exp3, "4.58 against 0.26 kJ"). The brief's language rule on "first" turned three ordinal uses into "opening".

D185. The journals and the build. The guides for authors of the Journal of Power Sources, the Journal of Energy Storage and the Fire Safety Journal, the first journal's good practice guide for battery papers, and the three editorial boards were read at source on 20 November 2026 (`manuscript/rebuild/journal_rules_20261120.md`); none invites pre-submission enquiries or gives an address for them, and none requires an institutional e-mail. The manuscript meets the strictest limits where they are limits (`compliance.md`): 4,929 words of main text and 342 of appendices, 7 figures and 3 tables, a 198-word abstract, five highlights of at most 84 characters, seven keywords, numbered sections, SI units, figures as vector PDF and 1,000 dpi TIFF in the Okabe and Ito colours, the funding sentence in the guides' words, and the generative AI section's heading for the author to write (his instruction of 11 November). It does not meet the Fire Safety Journal's preference for 20 double-spaced pages (it is 33), and, for the Journal of Energy Storage, Supplementary Sections S5 to S7 would move into an appendix; both are left to the main chat. The Word files are made from the LaTeX sources by `manuscript/rebuild/build_word.py` (pandoc with the Elsevier numeric style from the Citation Style Language project, CC BY-SA 3.0, in `build/`), with the numbers of sections, figures, tables and equations read from the LaTeX build's .aux files; the PDFs are the LaTeX ones, since LibreOffice leaves Word equations out of the PDFs it writes. The graphical abstract (`src/make_rebuild_ga.py`) draws the two thresholds of the two tests where both are bracketed with a strong trigger, the shortfall with conduction alone, the history's 37 K and the least path bound, every label traced. Two datasets of the reference list are marked [dataset], as the guides ask.
